Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the universe object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 677. The moduli space has dimension at most 431 with 3 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to universe theory, we decompose the problem into 677 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 431. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
